### **Notes** {#notes}

* Bullet points prefixed with ‘(Roll20 Chat)’ were expressed in chat instead of in voice. If it does not have the ‘(Roll20 Chat)’ prefix, it was collected from voice.  Things expressed in chat are generally in-character unless mentioned otherwise. It may be background injections into the topic / conversation that is on voice, a side roleplay, or background banter, or just individualized character expression, thought, or background actions / reactions .

### **Session 0 – Initial Team Formation** {#session-0-–-initial-team-formation}

* Session 0 / 0.5, Day 0.5, Initial Night, Saturday  
* Roster: Up-Tempo, Quasar-Force, Robin, Lock-Step, Magius  
* Real World Session date: 2026-05-14  
* In-World date: 2026-10-17  
* Up-Tempo does some lone superheroism, but is otherwise a quiet night before bedtime.  
* Robin’s player leaves the campaign between session 0 and 1  
* Oracle’s player joins the campaign between session 0 and 1

### **Session 1 – Rescue Robin \#1** {#session-1-–-rescue-robin-#1}

* Session 1, Day 1, Sunday (first full-day)  
* Real World Session date: 2026-05-21  
* In-World Date: 2026-10-18  
* Roster: Up-Tempo, Oracle, Quasar-Force, Lock-Step, Magius  
* Qausar-Force intuits Red Aegis Remnant as the likely abductors of Robin since Darkwing said in a file-briefing that they will come for them particularly Lucian.   
* Infiltrate Red Aegis Remnant base.  
  * Quasar-Force gets one-shotted by Shadow-Boxer  
  * Quasar-Force is revived by Oracle  
  * Quasar-Force spends the rest of the combat using the vents for cover giving only minor telekinetic support  
  * Shadow-Boxer tries to recruit Lock-step  
* Find leads to a shipyard. It wasn’t Robin, but a different hero, Sportsmaster, that was captured and being tortured, rescued him  
  * Quasar-Force starts making use of the drones. The drone’s infrared imaging is awesome, effectively supersicence. Enables precise location of the captured subject  
  * Lock-Step botches a blind teleport and spoils the stealth, but regardless, manages to pull off the rescue of Sportsmaster without much trouble, delivering him to a temporarily safe area of the seaport.  
  * Some delay from determining who is retrieving the rescued person and retreating with him. Oracle ultimately   
  * Quasar-Force is the last to leave, almost gets captured by Hijinx but manages to telekinetically wrestle himself out of her grab. His high-speed flight enables his getaway and easily catches up with the group.  
* Quasar-Force’s drone surveillance finds where Robin is actually at.  
* Magius’ player leaves the campaign between session 1 and 2  
* Intrepid’s player joins the campaign between session 1 and 2

### **Session 2 – Rescue Robin \#2** {#session-2-–-rescue-robin-#2}

* Session 2, Day 2, Monday  
* Roster: Up-Tempo, Oracle, Intrepid, Quasar-Force, Lock-Step, Sportsmaster (NPC)  
* In-World date: 2026-10-19  
* Real World Session date: 2026-05-28  
* At a church-like location. Robin tied up and prepped for ritual sacrifice. The ritual was ongoing, being performed by Red Aegis boss and many minions..  
* Smashed (don’t remember by who \- probably Intrepid or Oracle) enabled Quasar-Force to get line-of-sight to the boss  
* Intrepid was an incredible tank  
* Quasar Force got a major telekinetic grab out of the boss and did a lot of damage.  
* Oracle wanted to smash through the window. Everybody started arguing over how to do the rescue with some wanting to just charge in as Oracle suggested. Lock-Step just teleported into the place, ignoring the whole argument.  
* Everyone else followed through with the ‘window smashing storm the castle’ momentum.  
* Lock-Step stunned the boss for a round, allowing Quasar-Force to maintain his grab and crush him.  
* Lock-Step teleported Robin from the sacrifice table while the boss was distracted.  
* Once the boss broke out of the stun, he obscured visibility in the area, making Quasar-Force unable to maintain his telekinetic grab.  
* Everyone else was pre-occupied with minions, there were a lot of them.  
* Intrepid took down the batch of minions blocking his advance to the boss, and got the incap ‘kill’ on the boss, who was already very injured from Quasar-Force’s telekinesis.  
* Sportsmaster got pretty beaten up, and got mentally broken (but not shattered) via focus-drain.  
  * Oracle’s Heal and Quasar-Force’s Barrier kept Sportsmaster from being fully incapacitated from health-loss and also the barrier prevented Sportsmaster from getting fully shattered from focus-drain.  
* Quasar-Force managed to keep himself in cover (tree canopy) and not get significant enemy attention.  
* Up-Tempo’s player leaves the campaign between session 2 and 3  
* Exemplar’s player joins the campaign between session 2 and 3

### **Session 3 – Intervene on Looming Gang War between Burnout Kings and Insidious** {#session-3-–-intervene-on-looming-gang-war-between-burnout-kings-and-insidious}

* Session 3, Day 3, Tuesday  
* Real World Session date: 2026-06-04  
* In-World date: 2026-10-20  
* Roster: Exemplar, Oracle, Intrepid, Quasar-Force, Lock-Step  
* Started out to be mostly talk and threats, something that could be intervened with via diplomacy.  
* It got third-partied by a group of supervillains calling themselves the Destined, portraying themselves as violent vigilante iron-age 90s anti-heroes.  
* The Destined are very sadistic killers. Brutally murdered most of the burnout kings gang members in two rounds.  
* Quasar-Force got a close-up view on two of the brutal murders, likely by an invisible Fadeout, one of them a very gory bisection of the torso.  
* Another supervillain, Ravenwing, put out black tendrils and sucked the life out of one of the burnout kings, and shattered the mind of another.  
* Quasar-Force is able to halt the brutal murder of some of the burnout kings, at least the ones being murdered by Fade-Out, via a telekinetic barrier  
* One of the intervening supervillains, Shroud, blacks-out the area, which very much affects Quasar-force  
* The telepathic supervillain, Viral, attempts to assault Quasar-Force’s mind, but only managing to only get basic prerequisite telepathic contact, classifying Quasar-Force’s mind as very resilient.  
* Quasar-force exploits his drone’s hyper-spectral vision to target Shroud through her darkness cloud, telekinetically grabs her and throws her out of the battlefield, but Shroud happens to have the mobility to quickly return to the battlefield quickly; she is able to teleport.  
* Quasar-Force made himself a target, at least by Hexaliber (was already being targeted by Viral), by showing his apparent ability to see through the darkness shroud, but managed to dodge his shots.  
* Intrepid gets an incredible hit on Hexcaliber, who was on the back-line with Viral, prompting the whole supervillain group to retreat via their teleporter, Shroud.  
* None of the Insidious gang members died, either they were intentionally not targeted, or they were just smart for promptly running away while simultaneously being very lucky.  
* The third party intervention and mass-murder was well-captured on drone footage, and via Quasar-Force, the evidence communicated with police and public superhero forums, as well as Nightwing. Quasar-force informs Nightwing of the ‘image-likeness’ of Fadeout to the Robin theme. Quasar-Force is disturbed by Fade-Out’s brutality.  
* Three of the burnout kings gang members survived, but one with a shattered mind and another seriously injured.

### **Session 4 – Iron-Side introduction and Bank Robbery** {#session-4-–-iron-side-introduction-and-bank-robbery}

* Session 4, Day 4, Wednesday  
* Real World Session date: 2026-06-11  
* In-World date: 2026-10-21  
* Roster: Exemplar, Oracle, Intrepid, Quasar-Force, Lock-Step  
* The Iron-Side introduction was in the entire morning and the early afternoon of day 4, until about 2:30 to 3:00 PM (when public schools let out). The bank robbery takes place in the late afternoon, 4 to 6 PM.  
* Intrepid engage a supervillain, Hijinx, in the vault  
* Exemplar engages the supervillain in the lobby, Shadow Boxer  
* Oracle takes down Insidious gang members and gives some support to Exemplar.  
* Lock-Step teleport-outs hostages with extreme effectiveness.  
* Quasar-Force floats upside down outside the bank’s main entrance and removes guns from Insidious ang members (forcibly disarming them via telekinesis and tossing them out of the building out to the police lines). Gets Shadow Boxer’s attention who disengages from Exemplar and tries to throw a ranged attack at Quasar-Force, but Quasar then retreats higher into the air and Shadow-Boxer narrowly avoids getting telekinetically grappled, which he then goes back inside and suggests retreat due to most of the Insidious ang members being incapacitated or disarmed.  
* The Insidious gang members were very determined not to surrender, suspicious of them not being completely of free will.  
* Lock Step starts helping in the lobby after he finishes teleporting out the hostages  
* Shadow-Boxer claims to Lock-Step he got a better paying employer  
* Exemplar and Oracle go to the vault to assist Intrepid, who was barely holding ground against Hijinx. Hijinkx was eventually incapacitated and captured, though it is expected she will escape custody because of her powerset.  
* Shadow-Boxer gets evacuated by Shroud from the murderous vigilante / supervillain group, the Destined.  
* The bank robbery, after the group’s arrival, mostly from the lobby perspective, was well captured via drone footage  
* Quasar-Force and Lock-Step were the most publicly visible heroes during the bank robbery scene, Quasar-Force primarily media footage as he was outside the bank building the whole time floating upside down in his art-mural biosuit, Lock-step primarily via hostage word-of-mouth / testimony and some media footage (teleporting outside with hostages). Exemplar was the third most visible being the most visible inside the bank from the front entrance engaging with Shadow-Boxer.. Oracle and Intrepid had minimal public visibility.  
* Speculation: Shadowboxer and Hijinx defected from Red Aegis and joined the Destined.  
* Speculation: Probability becoming higher that the Insidious gang is being patronized or manipulated by the Destined, and the Murderous vigilante is a facade and the Destined are true supervillains.

### **Session 5 – Iron-Side And Lex-Corp Warehouse** {#session-5-–-iron-side-and-lex-corp-warehouse}

* Session 5, Day 5 Thursday to Day 7 Saturday  
* Real World Session date: 2026-06-25  
* In-World date: 2026-10-22 to 2026-10-24  
* Roster: Exemplar, Oracle, Intrepid, Quasar-Force, Lock-Step  
* Iron-Side and School, Day 5, 6, and morning / afternoon of day 7  
  * Iron-Side is instituting structured wake-up time, and when able (no superhero work intruding), structured bedtime. Things are predictable for Oracle making breakfast for Quasar-Force. Quasar-Force falls in-line with the structured wake-up and bedtime.  
  * Iron-side is teaching a lot of ‘school’ and a variety of topics. Mostly mandatory education type stuff.  
  * Iron-Side is very disciplined and methodical in her teaching structure  
  * The curriculum is customized for the individual heroes, and there is imposed cross-mentoring in groups of two or three  
    * Group 1: Exemplar, Intrepid  
    * Group 2: Oracle, Quasar-Force, Lock-Step  
  * Initially gave homework but stopped because of superhero work interference. Instead institutes an hour-long mandatory study / homework period, ‘small-classroom study-hall’ where study is enforced.  
  * Iron-Side does not appoint a leader or try to impose a chain of command. Iron-side seems to be pretty chill outside of being highly professional with her education / tutoring.  
  * Iron-Side’s imposition of discipline and structure is limited to her fulfilling her duty in providing sufficient education to the group, though seems to be encouraging more social meshing with the cross-mentoring.  
  * Quasar-Force does comparative research on Iron-Side. Finds her to be very similar to school teachers / tutors hired by sports teams or Hollywood studios when they have multiple underage athletes or actors. Different from e-sports kids who usually attend remote online-school of dubious quality. Quasar-Force thinks Iron-Side is mostly there to tend to their compulsory education as everyone except for Exemplar are supposed to be enrolled in high school, and Quasar-Force & Lock-Step are still of compulsory education age.  
  * Iron-Side is not actively engaging with the team unless engaged first. Was accommodating with Lock-Step knocking on her door though.  
* Video Games and Convincing to go to the Party, Afternoon Day 7  
  * Because of the lack of availability of his friends, Lock-Step ends up playing video games in front of the big-screen TV watching anime and movies, and playing video games.  
  * Intrepid spends a fair bit of time playing video games with Lock-Step, mostly sports games.  
  * Intrepid is skittish with playing the video games, worried about breaking the controllers.  
  * Lock-Step sucks at the NBA sports game and gives up and hands the controller over to Quasar-Force when he sees him flying by.  
  * Quasar-Force flies by the videos games on the screen, sees the controllers occupied, and just before going back to the lab, Lock-Step hands over the controller to Quasar-Force telling him the controller is broken  
  * Quasar-Force instantly picks up the somewhat-unfamiliar NBA game (he knows FIFA though). Even plays working the controller with TK.  
  * Lock-Step sees how well Quasar-Force was performing at the game, and invites competitive players he knows online to go against him.  
  * Quasar-Force gets nervous and botches the performance against the semi-pro competitive players, but is not a complete embarrassment but does lose. His tactics were amazing even if his execution was lacking.  
  * Lock-Step pushes for everyone to go to a party. He knows a friend who has a yacht, a big show-off of his wealth. The Yacht is 120 feet in size.  
  * Quasar-Force agrees to go to the party but says he won’t drink any of the stupid-joice (alcohol). Lock-Step tells Quaar-Force not to drink anything they give him, which he has no issue going along with.  
  * Exemplar initially opts-out of the party. He wants to tend to his investigation office, move materials out of the office into Titans HQ. He is labelled by Lock-Step as a boring grown-up.  
  * Lock-step eventually gets Exemplar to agree to go after offering to help with the office move and convincing Quasar-Force to help too. Quasar Force’s telekinesis makes the move very easy.  
  * Lock-Step takes Quasar-Force to ask Iron-Side about using powers in public. Lock-Step thinks they aren't allowed to use powers in public, but admits he hasn’t ‘read the handbook’. Quasar-Force doesn’t know of any handbook, and says he’s never been told by Nightwing or Iron-Side not to use powers in public. Quasar-force has never hidden his power use from the public. She says generally ok as long as not causing damage or disturbance. With ‘she didn’t say No’ now ‘She definitely said Yes’, the positive answer gives Quasar-Force positive motivation to go to the party. Boosted by Lock-Step saying he will do ‘underwater flying’ with him and Lock-Step encouraging him to ‘fly the yacht’.  
  * Lock-Step convinces the remaining team members to go to the party.  
* Party, Evening Day 7  
  * The party mostly has teenagers 15-19, some young adults 20-24, zero full-adults (25+).. Moderate amount of alcohol, no drugs.  
  * Quasar-Force is the only under-15 at the party and the only one that looks pre-pubescent.  
  * The party has two 15-year-olds other than Lock-Step. Most partygoers are 16-18. The party’s host is 17\.  
  * Quasar-Force does surprisingly well at the party, at least by what was expected for him to be able to do, mostly via being honest and open and answering questions, especially technical computer questions.  
  * Lock-Step does amazing with socializing and winning friends / influencing people at the party.  
  * Exemplar ends up a complete wallflower at the party. Everyone else does pretty well socializing at the party.  
  * Quasar-Force wears a matching soccer jersey and shorts, stadium-authentic uniform of the pro soccer team in Metropolis, over his nanosuit at Lock-Step’s request. The nanosuit is a shorty so it appears as compression-wear under the soccer jersey & shorts. Lock Step asks if Quasar-Force has anything else, and he says before he got his powers, soccer jerseys / shorts were all he wore.  
  * Quasar-Force ‘flashes’ his nanosuit via pulling up his jersey while talking to people answering questions about his powers. He does not proactively share about his powers but shows no restraint in sharing when people ask.  
  * Quasar-Force achieves his main purpose for going to the party \- Lock-step peered pressured the party’s host (who does not own the yacht but his dad probably does) into consenting.  
  * Little bit of a rough landing, ‘letting go’ while still two feet above the water, but otherwise pulls off a great flight, 500 or so feet into the air, and gets the ‘ET silhouette’ photo of the flying yacht over the moon. The party goers are ecstatic.  
  * Quasar-Force wanted to go ‘flying’ under the ocean (Lock-Step agreed to go with him) but forgets with the exhilaration of the yacht-flight and the general momentum of the party, and the interruption of the wrist-communicator call for the call from help from the Lex-Corp warehouse.  
  * Quasar-Force was the only one wearing his duty wrist-communicator, mainly because he forgot to take it off before going to the party.  
* Warehouse, Late Evening Day 7  
  * Everyone responded promptly from the call for help that Quasar-Force got on his duty wrist-communicator, from a lex-corp warehouse while at the party. The party host is bummed about people leaving early. Lock-step shows off a teleport. Quasar-Force, already hovering, flashes his nanosuit by lifting his jersey.  
  * Everyone leaves promptly, but Quasar-Force, and Lock-step return to the ship thinking they may have forgotten Oracle, but Intrepid has already picked up Oracle on his way out. Quasar-Force forgets to remove his soccer uniform, so he ends up wearing it over his nanosuit into combat.  
  * Lock-Step engages the guard. The guard seems un-motivated but threatens to ‘arrest’ Lock-Step for tresspassing..  
  * Quasar-Force thinks he is just stalling for time but since he is not actively attacking, prefers to just ignore him, knows people are calling for help, rules the guard is just stalling for time, and flies up to the roof  
  * Lock-Step starts casting a spell, which provokes the guard.  
  * Quasar-Force TK-grabbed the guard and knocks him out with a squeeze from on top of the roof before Lock-step could finish his spell  
  * Party moves in, two mooks are seen, sporting Insidious gang colors  
  * Oracle shoots at a ganger while running in and the gangers swarm-in on Oracle. Pushes past  the guards and takes another shot. Oracle’s predictive module precognitions a supervillain will come out of a doorway down the alleyway, and broadcasts the warning over Exemplar’s mindlink.  
  * Exemplar and Intrepid engage one of the gangers in melee, Intrepid using ‘Banging Heads’ to hit both of them, and smashes both of them..  
  * Quasar-Force gets a massive telekinetic grab and crush on the supervillain that comes out. The supervillain uses energy absorption to absorb the kinetic energy of the crush. Then shoots at Quasar-Force with beams from her eyes, which he nimbly dodges like a pro with telekinetically assisted nimbleness while hovering in the air.  
  * Lock-Step attacks the supervillain with his vibranium chain whip, she erects a force-field, but immediately collapses under the attack.  
  * Exemplar and Intrepid engage the villain in melee. Intrepid totally rocks her world (Marvel).  
  * Quasar-Force gets the KO with a combined telekinetic strike and telekinetic crush. The crush was spectacular (Marvel). The supervillain can not use energy absorption because it can not be used again until the energy (focus) gained from its last used is consumed / used-up.  
  * The group is self-assessing they are working well as a team now, and Lock-step credits ‘All we had to do is go to a party’  
  * Three more supervillains and two more gangers come around the corner after the group finds what appears to be a testing / experimentation room with a busted-open containment cage. They get a jump on the whole party except for Oracle (most of them rolled marvels on their initiative).  
  * The supervillain with the hammer throws the hammer at Exemplar and Oracle. Glancing hit on Exemplar, misses Oracle by margin of his predictive module.  
  * Oracle focuses on the ganger mooks.  
  * The female supervillain with the superman symbol ignores the front-liners and moves towards Lock-Step and shoots him with heat-vision and misses. Lock-Step asks her if she could just surrender and offers her some cuffs and just cuff herself.. She says she is not into the kinky stuff.  
  * Lock-Step hits her with his Chain-Whip, lands a solid hit. Exemplar asks Lock-step if he needs help back there  
  * Quasar-Force tries to TK-grab the superman symbol supervillain girl but despite his best effort, she dodges like a pro.  
  * The male non-hammer supervillain turns into a giant green gorilla.  
  * The supervillain with the hammer does another hammer-AoE on Quasar-Force, Oracle, and Intrepid, and hits everyone except Oracle (missing Oracle by margin of his predictive module). Quasar is protected by his nanosuit and isn’t too badly damaged.  
  * The two gangers attack Exemplar and he gets hit by one of them. Oracle takes one of them out from range, and Exemplar hits the other but doesn’t knock him out. Intrepid tries to hit the hammer-wielding supervillain but misses.  
  * Superman-symbol girl tries to punch Lock-step but he blinks out of the way. She then rushes past Lock-Step. Lock-Step urges her to ‘just take the cuffs’ Lock-Step teleports up to her and attacks her with chain strikes via his Vibranium chain whip. Lock-Step scores a hit with assistance from Quasar-Force’s TK-nudge predictive assistance from Oracle. Lock-Step’s hit paralyzes the superman-sign female villain.  
  * Quasar-Force grabs the superman-symbol girl, succeeds because of paralysis, but she is just too tough for the the crush to do anything.  
  * The supervillain that turned into a giant gorilla punches at Oracle.and gets a modest hit.  
  * The hammer supervillain throws his hammer at Lock-Step to try to help the superman-symbol supervillain girl and gets a moderate hit, moves up to get both Lock-step and Quasar-Force in range of his hammer.  
  * Exemplar engages the superman-symbol female villain.  
  * Intrepid attacks the giant green gorilla supervillain, who then phases out causing his fist to just go through him as if it were vapor.  
  * The one remaining ganger goes up to track Oracle, but misses and Oracle gets a riposte attack, and takes him down.  
  * Lock-Step teleports at the hammer-wielding supervillain, who immediately attacks him, but misses. Lock-Step then paralyzes him and  Quasar-Force hits him with a telekinetic strike.  
  * The giant green gorilla supervillain punches Intrepid and scores a decent hit, ignoring his toughness because the fist phased into his body. Intrepid punches back at him, and his fist just goes into his phased body and does nothing.  
  * Exemplar punches at superman-symbol girl and misses wildly, who then counter-attacks and misses.  
  * Lock-Step reconfigures his powerful hex from the ‘real jinx’ power to the ‘disrupt person’ power, the same power that the giant green gorilla supervillain is using, and scores a spectacular hit taking out half of his health. The hammer supervillain throws his hammer with a reaction and misses.  
  * Quasar-Force pins the hammer supervillain with telekinesis, but fails to do any damage with the crush.  
  * The giant green gorilla supervillain punches Intrepid again with his phased fist, and then moves towards the hammer-wielding supervillain.  
  * The hammer-wielding supervillain attacks everyone except Lock-Step (who is too far away) with his hammer AoE. Quasar-Force barely dodges it with his telekinetically supercharged agility, and hits everyone else.  
  * Lock-Step proposes retreat. Quasar-Force wants to focus on Hammer villain. Lock-Step agrees.  
  * Intrepid tries to punch at the hammer-wielding supervillain and misses.  
  * The soccer uniform Quasar-Force forgot to take off before combat is still intact as he has only modestly been hit (twice) by the hammer-villain’s hammer. It hasn’t come across his mind yet that he has forgotten to remove the soccer jersey shorts he wore to the party over his nanosuit.  
  * Session ends, combat resumes next session.  
* Rank-Up to Rank 3.1 (using optional ‘Getting Schooled’ incremental rank advancement rule)

### **Session 6 – Lex-Corp Warehouse Continued, Insidious Nest, Ganger / Supervillain Interrogation** {#session-6-–-lex-corp-warehouse-continued,-insidious-nest,-ganger-/-supervillain-interrogation}

* Session 6, Day 7 Saturday to Day 10 Tuesday  
* Real World Session date: 2026-07-02  
* In-World date: 2026-10-24 to 2026-10-27  
* Roster: Exemplar, Oracle, Intrepid, Quasar-Force, Lock-Step  
* Lock-Step’s player did not attend the session. He was NPC’d for the remainder of the warehouse scene and then the GM declared him to be spending several days with his cousin to take the character out of the session’s remaining scenes. This leave is assumed to be authorized.  
* Lex-Corp Warehouse Fight Continued  
  * Day 7 Saturday, Evening  
  * The hammer-throwing villain throws his hammer at Oracle as he moves towards him and gets a modest hit. Oracle gets a hit on the hammer-wielding supervillain and stuns him.  
  * Exemplar gets a hit on the hammer-wielding supervillain and drains focus from him  
  * The superman-symbol supervillainess goes straight after lock-step and attempts to heat-vision him but misses due to his blink. His blink triggers a reflexive hammer throw from the hammer-throwing supervillain, but it misses.  
  * Quasar-Force gets a telekinetic grab on the hammer-throwing villain and gets a good squeeze on him  
  * The giant green gorilla supervillain has a sudden morale failure from the hammer-thrower’s critical health and Lock-Step’s phasing-fist and takes the unconscious first supervillain and runs.  
  * The hammer-throwing supervillain hits everyone with the hammer in an AoE. Oracle is in critical health (4hp remaining)  
  * Lock-Step gets a hit on the hammer-throwing supervillain.  
  * Intrepid knocks-out the hammer throwing supervillain.  
  * Quasar-Force tells the group to stay out of melee with the superman-symbol supervillain. Exemplar approaches her anyway, healing himself on the way.

Name Cornwall DeLaforrest. Likely to be in the top 10-20 richest people in Bludhaven. CFO of the local Star Labs Branch in Bludhaven (headquartered in titans tower). The facilities are very protected from drone surveillance.

Extrapolative models compromised. External information detected. Not supposed to get externally fed information. Somebody just introduced a variable. Voice, unidentifiable, young woman’s voice, not synthesized, not static, not a recording, not internally generated, “please”, disappears. Systems back to normal. No intrusion, malware, telepathy detected.

Each one is connected, moving to earth. Celestial Core stable, 98% normal. Projected status uncontained. Time Unknown. Event classification, Theft 91%, Metahuman Incursion 96%, Containment Failure 73%,. Mass Casualty Event 68%, Biological Event 99.8%. Pathogen no match, toxin no match, Parasite inconclusive.

Event window 12 hours, Location Star Labs lower facility, Primary risk unknown, recommendation intervention. Text is displayed in a field that doesn’t belong:“Don’t let us leave together”

      * The celestial core going critically unstable will take out half of Bludhaven. If both the star heart and the celestial core interact when going unstable it could affect the stability of the universe.

    * Intrepid steps up to the fourth individual (the breeder) and misses  
    * Psyche commands Quasar-Force to attack the force field, succeeds on the mental command with a marvel (giving an edge on the next command attempt). The main force field is taken down. An auxiliary force field remains but it is much weaker.  
    * Exemplar moves up to the breeder and would have missed even with Intrepid’s assist, but Quasar-Force’s telekinetic nudge (because the command didn’t consume his reactions) put the punch on-target, and deals moderate damage  
    * The breeder moves towards the unconscious Star Heart near the reactor and lays down web trapping on Quasar-FOrce, Intrepid, Exemplar, Oracle, and Doctor Varga  
    * Lock-Step got the message at a BBQ and got boom-tubed to Titans Tower, who then teleports into the Star Labs facility using drone footage feed for visualization.  
    * Lock-Step teleports in, Quasar-Force recognizes him from within the webbing. Lock-Step recognizes Quasar-Force, manages to barely understand what Quasar-Force was saying, moves over to the unconscious Star Heart, grabs at him, but the Breeder interposes, and gets grabbed instead, and then uses slip-free to automatically escape from Lock-Step’s grasp.  
    * (Round 5\) Oracle escapes his webbing, gets back into line of sight of Psyche, hits her with suppressive fire for moderate focus damage.  
    * Fuego emits a hellfire elemental supernova and hits everyone for moderate damage (low-moderate for Intrepid and Oracle who have higher damage reduction)  
    * Intrepid escapes his webbing, and punches Fuego’s hellfire sphere and utterly destroys it, and then moves up to Fuego to stare him down the face.  
    * Psyche unleashes a telepathic blast onto Quasar-Force doing major focus damage, draining him from 130 to 90 remaining focus.  
    * Quasar-Force grabs the unconscious and moves him away from the Breeder and tells Lock-Step to get him out.  
    * Exemplar fails to escape the webbing with the free agility based escape attempt, but manages to use his action to use his brute strength to tear out of the webbing. Moves to the Breeder after Quasar-Force tells him try to obstruct the Breeder  
    * The Breeder attempts a spider-strike against Exemplar and Lock-Step and misses them both. Jumps towards Stear-Heart to be ready to interpose against a repeat grab attempt by Lock-Step  
    * Lock-Step teleports towards the breeder and swings his chain-whip, uses combat trickery to get an automatic Marvel, and knocks out the breeder. Then teleports away from the brawl.  
    * (Round 6\) Oracle moves up to line of sight of Fuego. Fuego shows signs of returning to himself. Oracle stands by for further hostility. Psyche comes-to also. Apparently the breeder going unconscious, caused the others to be released from his control.

* Pow Wow with Nightwing  
  * Day 39 Wednesday, late-morning to early-afternoon  
  * Star Labs runs residual test to make sure everyone is clean and has no residual issues  
  * Star Labs gets in touch with Nightwing.  
  * Iron-Side wants to get updated with what went on  
  * Lock-Step is permitted to go back to theBBQ  
  * Lock-Step: “Alright, so like, uh, you know, I'll see you. You know, just call me on the communicator, you know, between two and eight I'm kind of busy. But if you must, you know, just call. Oh, and, you know, stay safe. Don't get mind-controlled, Quasar.”  
  * Lock-Step teleports away.  
  * Iron-Side: “it looks like you were safely able to handle this situation. I understand, uh, from Guy, I got an earful from Mr. Gardner about paying you kids.” She smiles, "So I assume Lockstep joined you?”  
  * Nightwing is on speakerphone / teleconference in Iron-Side’s office  
  * Quasar-Force: “Yeah, Lockstep wants money. Uh, you need to tell me more about this mother box and me. Multiple people say you harbor all kinds of secrets on it, including the guy... and, well, Lockstep got it from somewhere. Obviously your secret's not a secret anymore.“  
  * Nightwing: “Well, your technology is from a place called Apokolips. Uh, it is also from... New Genesis. So basically, there's these aliens out there who think of themselves as gods, and they created these mother boxes that are designed to be these intelligent supercomputers. Uh, that are able to exert a bit of control over space and time, creating things like boom tubes, other technological advances like that. The box that you have is some kind of variant of that technology. We haven't been able to fully determine what it is, mostly because it's kind of fused with your genetics. So, you think we've got secrets from you, but really the box itself has secrets from you and from us.“  
  * Quasar-Force: “Well, it showed me what happened to it. They were dissecting her, literally.”  
  * Nightwing: “So I mean, they are part intelligent creatures, part highly developed machines. You know, and they're about the size of a brick or so. But yeah, they... created by a New Genesis scientist named Himon. Using a specific element which is very rare in the universe. Essentially portable supercomputers, miniaturized. They're really not completely known, they manipulate energy and like I said, alter space so you can do teleportation. They're known to have healing properties. But it is a technology that is beyond the understanding of those who created it, much less us here on Earth. So it's not like we're completely keeping secrets from you or anything like that, it's just we don't really know a whole lot about your box. We know about the other mother boxes, but that doesn't necessarily translate one-to-one correlation with what happened with you and what this thing is."  
  * Quasar-Force: “Are there other mother boxes on the earth?”  
  * Nightwing: “Oh, very rare that they come to Earth. Most of them are on New Genesis, a few of them are on Apokolips.”  
  * Quasar-Force: “But you seem to know a few of them.“  
  * Nightwing: “Yeah, I mean Orion uses it to control his rage. Mr. Miracle has one. Yeah, there's a few of them out there. But they're fairly rare. Usually have an affinity for the Source, which I guess is a great mystical energy... that gives the New Gods their powers. So yeah, I mean it's a bunch of sci-fi hoodoo if you ask me, but that's what we know about it. So if you consider knowing that and not telling you keeping secrets, then I guess we have. I guess we should apologize for that “  
  * Quasar-Force: “Thank you for what you're telling me what you told me so far... Guy's gonna send me a whole bunch of stuff on my box.”

  * Quasar-Force Relic Research (23-normal logic check)  
  * The museum people believe that the Aegis Fang was actually the legendary Athena's weapon. Diana (Wonder Woman) said it wasn’t.  
  * They are calling the exhibit the Daughters of War. And it is being sub-labeled as the Amazons, the Olympians, and the Lost Kingdom of Themyscira. They are advertising the centerpiece exhibit, a collection of recently authenticated Greek, Anatolian, and Black Sea antiquities exploring the relationship between the historical warrior women of antiquity, the Amazons of Greek legend, and their supposedly divine patrons. Curators basically think they're presenting a sort of mythology. Um, and there's plenty of genuine mythology behind the idea of the exhibit. The ancient tradition makes the Amazons the daughters of Ares and a nymph called Harmonia associated with the region around Themyscira. Athena also has strong iconography connecting to both the Aegis and the Amazonian. The legendary wars between the Greeks and the Amazons were famously depicted on monuments associated with Athens including the Parthenon. The artifacts are in the central gallery. The museum basically has the three prizes together, in addition to some other items that basically are supposedly Amazonian or ancient Greek. They're mostly picking them out because of the motifs of stars, crescents, and owls, other geometry, and archaic Greek inscriptions. Basically, it's a very lovely display that they have. The other items... most of them are actually later dated basically. More like descendants of these three items. Pieces of armor and jewelry and stuff like that in there. The owl motif is why they're associating everything with Athena. And basically because everything was owl-based with her.. None of the other artifacts outside of the main three have any supernatural or magical properties.

### **Session 15 – The Watchtower Continued, Exiled Progenitor, Gray Lanterns** {#session-15-–-the-watchtower-continued,-exiled-progenitor,-gray-lanterns}

* Session 15, Day 46 Wednesday to Day 56 Saturday  
* Roster: Exemplar II, Oracle, Intrepid, Quasar-Force, Lock-Step  
* In-World date: 2026-12-02 to 2026-12-12  
* Real World Session date: 2026-09-24  
* The session on 2026-09-17 did not occur because too many players could not attend.  
* Exemplar II’s player left the campaign between session 14 and 15  
* This session was 100% roleplay and had a lot of deep lore. There was potential for combat, but Lock-Step turned it into a diplomatic encounter.  
* Watchtower Doctor Tal Taleria Continued  
  * Day 46 Wednesday, Late-Morning  
  * Doctor Tal Taleria: “I need to correct the language everyone around you has apparently been using, or several people in response to you at any rate. You do not have a Mother Box. And she is not inside you. Those statements describe an object and its owner, this is not what I observe. You can find her everywhere.”  
    * She takes a scanner and basically turns it on like a display towards Endo.  
    * “It looks like a kind of biological but branching network so tightly threaded through your cells, nerves, bones, tissue. I cannot point to the place where you end and she begins. And for the record, I am going to call her she unless either of you objects. The Mother Boxes are people. Peculiar people, but people. Yeah, she's a person and she looks over at you and goes, and that was directed at you. Yes, you. I know you're listening. Humans, they persist in calling them computers. New Gods occasionally make the same mistake. Although they're considerably less of an excuse. Mother Box thinks, she feels, she forms attachments. She learns her user or perhaps her partner would be more appropriate and adapts to them. But even that description is inadequate. A Mother Box is technology constructed around a relationship with something your businesses would struggle to quantify the source. That little sound you probably heard describing as pinging isn't merely a notification tone, it is communication. Admittedly, Mother Boxes are not always verbose conversationalists, and yours less so since she was so damaged.”  
    * She looks at Endo with a little bit of sadness in her eyes.  
    * “Asmuth did not invent your Mother Box. He acquired her. He began a project. He began as a product of New Genesis at some point. She passed through Apokoliptian hands and was altered there. Eventually, Asmuth obtained her. He was obsessed with the Source. Not worship, not understanding. He wanted to control it. He believed Mother Boxes possessed something we lacked an intimacy with the Source. So he began trying to determine why. You’re 14\. I don't think you really need to know the extent of the experiments he performed, nor does it obligate me to describe the torture to you in clinical details. I'm fairly certain she.

* WatchTower Time-Skip  
  * Day 46 Wednesday, early afternoon to Day 52 Evening  
  * The group is not allowed outside of the guest quarters floor of theWatchtower,but they do get a full run of the entire floor.  
  * Quasar-Force works in the lab / workshop all week working on the signal masking device and components for enhancing the anti-teleport security at Titans Tower. Quasar-Force completely forgets about making a replica of the floating medical robot. Quasar-Force has a grand panoramic view of the earth from space blue marble from the common room window. The view helps Qujsar-Force feel serene and relaxed despite all the hard work he is doing. Quasar-Force pays no attention to his aerial posture, with no concern for which way is up unless he needs to interact with equipment on the floor.  
  * Lock-Step spends much of his time working up the group’s social media presence. Streams more in depth tours of the guest quarters floor of the Watchtower, and extensive coverage of the guest floor common area converted to a lab and workshop.  
  * Lock-Step’s friend, Sarah, gets a lot of insider footage of the guest quarters level of the Watthower, and preferential interaction / response on the comments. Sarah, as the tech expert of Lock-step’s group, gets jealous of the equipment Quasar-Force is getting to work with. Lock-Step decrees Sarah as his best friend.  
  * Sarah makes many social media posts repeating the footage of Quasar-Force, “look at the doodads Quasar-Force is rocking\! You got to come check this tower out\!”. Sarah pushes Lock-Step to bias Quasar-Force on the streaming footage. Quasar-Force is not camera shy, but he is also not entertaining it either unless Lock-Step prompts him to. He is zoned into his work.  
  * Lock-Step and Sarah put up a show to help the virality of the stream, playing a jealousy game and manipulating the audience to envy the Watchtower accommodations, with Sarah pushing to emphasize the technology aspect (and via that,Quasar-Force). Lock-Step is leveraging Quasar-Force to get him his ‘bragging rights’ Quasar-Force doesn’t care, he is absorbed in the tech.  
  * Over the Time-Skip:  
    * Successfully build the signal masking device. It also includes mental defense fortification / augmentation to make it harder for enemy telepaths to mess with his mind.  
      * It provides New God signal masking for the Quasar-Force and the mother box inside, and also fortifies Quasar-Force’s mind with Uncanny 3, adding three levels of focus damage reduction, and (via GM hours rule) adding three to all of his mental defenses (vigilance, ego, logic), without the need to spend any focus or expend any actions / reactions.  
        * The circlet is made out of metamaterial-infused Hastelloy C-22. Plated with a gold / rhodium alloy. The circlet embeds new-god based circuitry to produce a combined matrix of passive cloaking and active jamming.  
    * Successfully build an anti-teleportation device that is effective with Ravenwing’s dimensional portal, and will learn and adapt other teleportation methodologies and should be effective within the second attempt.  
    * Quasar-Force’s nanosuit also fortifies from Sturdy 2 to Sturdy 3\. Producing a three-way collaborative effort between mother box, Quasar-Force, and Dr Taleria to improve Quasar-Force’s, and the mother box’s survivability.  
    * Wonder Woman successfully challenges Ecliptica and gets the Themysciran artifacts back (Diadem of Selene, Aegis Fang, sphere of Harmonia)  
  * After Wonder Woman comes back from the challenge and Quasar-Force finishes the signal masking device, the group is returned to Titans Tower.
